| The draft is shaped per channel: email with subject line reusing the application headline, LinkedIn message shorter with no subject, portal message plain text (.claude/commands/outcome.md:101) | followup.shape_for_channel | test_channel_shapes_subject_only_for_email | planned |
| The draft is iterated until the user is happy before anything is logged (.claude/commands/outcome.md:102) | followup.review_loop | test_draft_revision_loop_until_confirmed | planned |
| On user confirmation that they will send or have sent it, the log is written in the same turn: `followed up YYYY-MM-DD` appended to notes and the final text saved as `followup_YYYY-MM-DD.md` in the application archive (.claude/commands/outcome.md:104-107) | followup.log_sent | test_confirmed_followup_appends_marker_and_saves_file | planned |
| If the user decides not to send, nothing is logged (.claude/commands/outcome.md:109) | followup.log_sent | test_declined_followup_writes_nothing | planned |
| Termination: after two follow-ups and continued silence a third is never offered; the flow moves to recording a resolution and the user decides whether to mark no_response with the time since last contact shown and no imposed cutoff (.claude/commands/outcome.md:111; .claude/commands/outcome.md:242; tests/test_outcome_followup.py:59-65) | followup.candidates / followup.after_cap_prompt | test_third_followup_never_offered | planned |
| If the user reports an actual response (interview invitation, rejection) mid-branch, the flow drops out and records it through the normal outcome flow (.claude/commands/outcome.md:111) | followup.review_loop | test_response_mentioned_exits_branch_to_outcome_recording | planned |
| Follow-ups are draft only, never sent: the follow-up code has no mail, message or portal capability and must not be wired to tools that send (.claude/commands/outcome.md:240; tests/test_outcome_followup.py:43-49) | followup (module-level) | test_followup_module_imports_no_network_or_mail_client | planned (stricter: structural, no sender exists) |
| Thank-you notes: when an interview stage is newly ticked, a short thank-you note is offered in the same turn and, if accepted, drafted under the follow-up voice, no-new-claims and `followup_YYYY-MM-DD.md` rules (.claude/commands/outcome.md:189) | followup.thank_you_offer | test_newly_ticked_stage_triggers_thankyou_offer | planned |
| The thank-you trigger is the act of recording a stage; no scan of past stages is ever done (.claude/commands/outcome.md:189) | followup.thank_you_offer | test_thankyou_not_offered_for_already_ticked_stage | planned |
| Interview-prep guidance: thank-you note is brief (2-3 sentences) and expresses appreciation; do not call to stand out post-submission; respect an employer-stated timeline; a status call acceptable only after 2+ weeks with no timeline (.claude/skills/job-application-assistant/07-interview-prep.md:94-103) | followup.etiquette_rules | test_thankyou_is_two_to_three_sentences | planned |

### Stale sweep

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stale candidates are rows whose status is `applied` or `interview`, quiet longer than the threshold, measured from `date` or the latest dated note, whichever is later (.claude/commands/outcome.md:119) | stale.candidates | test_only_applied_and_interview_rows_qualify | planned |
| Drafted rows are strictly excluded from the sweep because they were never submitted and cannot receive a response (.claude/commands/outcome.md:119; tests/test_outcome_stale.py:126-131) | stale.candidates | test_drafted_rows_never_swept | planned |
| Default sweep threshold is 60 days; `stale <N>` or `sweep <N>` uses N (.claude/commands/outcome.md:25-26, .claude/commands/outcome.md:121; tests/test_outcome_stale.py:133-137) | stale.candidates | test_default_sixty_day_threshold_and_override | planned |
| Unparseable-date rows are skipped with a note in the sweep (.claude/commands/outcome.md:119) | stale.candidates | test_sweep_skips_unparseable_rows_with_note | planned |
| With no qualifying row the sweep reports that none exceed the threshold and stops without writing (.claude/commands/outcome.md:123-125) | stale.run | test_empty_sweep_reports_and_writes_nothing | planned |
| Candidates are shown as a numbered table (company, role, date applied, days quiet, follow-ups sent, current status, proposed `no_response`) (.claude/commands/outcome.md:127-136) | stale.present | test_sweep_table_columns_and_proposed_status | planned |
| Nothing is written until the user answers `all`, `select` (numbers like "1, 3" or "1-4") or `skip`; the sweep never resolves automatically (.claude/commands/outcome.md:138-146; .claude/commands/outcome.md:243; tests/test_outcome_stale.py:139-145) | stale.resolve | test_no_write_without_explicit_confirmation | planned (stricter: resolve() requires a confirmation token object) |
| `select` accepts comma lists and ranges and rejects out-of-range numbers (.claude/commands/outcome.md:143) | stale.parse_selection | test_selection_syntax_lists_ranges_and_bad_numbers | planned |
| `skip` cancels with no changes (.claude/commands/outcome.md:144) | stale.resolve | test_skip_makes_no_changes | planned |
| For each confirmed row, status becomes the canonical `no_response`, `stale resolved no_response (YYYY-MM-DD)` is appended to notes, all other columns preserved (.claude/commands/outcome.md:150; tests/test_outcome_stale.py:147-151) | stale.resolve / tracker.update_row | test_confirmed_row_gets_no_response_and_marker | planned |
| For each confirmed row with an existing archive folder, outcome.md gets `**Status:** no_response`, `**Date resolved:**` and a Notes line recording the stale resolution with days quiet and date (.claude/commands/outcome.md:151-154) | stale.resolve / archive.write_outcome | test_confirmed_row_updates_archive_outcome | planned |
| If 3 or more applications resolve in one sweep the calibration handoff is offered; otherwise a summary is shown and the flow stops (.claude/commands/outcome.md:156) | stale.run / calibrate.offer | test_calibration_offered_at_three_resolutions | planned |

|---|---|---|---|

| The fallback globs are never widened to the company alone, because two roles at one company would match and the first hit would win silently (.claude/commands/outcome.md:164; .claude/commands/interview.md:28) | archive.find_submitted | test_two_roles_same_company_do_not_cross_match | planned |
| If nothing matches (application made outside the apply flow) the file is skipped with a note; a sibling role's CV is never recorded as what was submitted (.claude/commands/outcome.md:164) | archive.archive_documents | test_missing_documents_skipped_with_note | planned |
| Files already in the archive are never overwritten; the archived version is what was actually submitted (.claude/commands/outcome.md:164; .claude/commands/outcome.md:236; .claude/commands/apply.md:407) | archive.archive_documents | test_existing_archive_files_never_overwritten | planned |
| An existing job_posting.md is left alone; otherwise the posting is fetched from the row's source URL (retrying a 403 with browser headers) and saved (.claude/commands/outcome.md:165) | archive.archive_posting / fetch | test_existing_posting_kept_and_fetch_retries_403 | planned |
| A dead posting URL gets a user-pasted copy or an explicit "unavailable" stub; a posting is never reconstructed from memory (.claude/commands/outcome.md:165; .claude/commands/outcome.md:237; .claude/commands/apply.md:407) | archive.archive_posting | test_dead_posting_yields_stub_never_reconstruction | planned |
| /apply archives the posting text it already holds verbatim at draft time (never a fresh fetch) and writes nothing if it no longer holds it (.claude/commands/apply.md:407) | archive.archive_posting_at_draft | test_draft_archives_held_posting_verbatim | planned |
| outcome.md has the exact format: title line `# Outcome: <Company> — <Role>`, a `**Status:**` line from the six-value archive enum, `**Date resolved:**` only when resolved, an "Interview stages reached" checklist (phone screen, technical, case, final round, offer received), and a Notes section (.claude/commands/outcome.md:168-185; documents/README.md:161-181) | archive.render_outcome / archive.parse_outcome | test_outcome_md_roundtrips_in_documented_format | planned |
| `in_progress` is the one addition to the archive enum, used for open applications; the date-resolved line is omitted while in progress (.claude/commands/outcome.md:173; .claude/commands/outcome.md:238; documents/README.md:183) | archive.render_outcome | test_in_progress_has_no_date_resolved | planned |
| Stage checkboxes are ticked as reached with a date in parentheses; Notes are appended with dates, never overwritten (.claude/commands/outcome.md:187) | archive.record_stage | test_stage_ticks_add_date_and_notes_append | planned |
| The archive Status changes from in_progress to a final value only on resolution (.claude/commands/outcome.md:187) | archive.record_outcome | test_status_only_leaves_in_progress_on_resolution | planned |
| Re-running the outcome record on the same application is idempotent: it appends new information and never duplicates folders, rows or history (.claude/commands/outcome.md:187; .claude/commands/outcome.md:239) | archive.record_outcome | test_recording_twice_is_idempotent | planned |
| Resolutions map to the archive enum: hired, offer_declined, rejected, no_response, interview_only (reached interviews but stalled or abandoned without explicit rejection) (.claude/commands/outcome.md:74-79) | archive.ARCHIVE_STATUSES | test_resolution_to_archive_status_mapping | planned |
| `no_response` is the user's decision: the time since last contact is shown, and no cutoff is imposed by the recording flow (.claude/commands/outcome.md:78; .claude/commands/outcome.md:111) | archive.record_outcome | test_no_response_requires_user_decision | planned |
| Feedback is recorded as the user reports it, verbatim where remembered, never fabricated (.claude/commands/outcome.md:83; .claude/commands/outcome.md:237) | archive.record_outcome | test_feedback_stored_verbatim | planned |
| Extra files (`interview_prep_<stage>.md`, `followup_YYYY-MM-DD.md`) may live in the folder and are ignored by the calibration reader, which reads only the four named files (documents/README.md:185; .claude/commands/outcome.md:107) | archive.read_for_calibration | test_calibration_reads_only_four_named_files | planned |
| The archive folder is personal data and gitignored (`documents/applications/**`), so nothing needs redacting (.claude/commands/outcome.md:162) | layout.ignore_rules | test_applications_dir_is_gitignored | planned |
| The tracker row is updated and the archive written in one recording step so the two stores agree (.claude/commands/outcome.md:5-6; .claude/commands/outcome.md:195) | archive.record_outcome / tracker.update_row | test_tracker_and_archive_updated_together | planned |
| The recorded tracker note is a short dated note (.claude/commands/outcome.md:195) | tracker.append_note | test_status_update_appends_dated_note | planned |
| Recording an upcoming or newly scheduled interview stage suggests preparing for it with the interview command (.claude/commands/outcome.md:223-225) | archive.record_stage | test_new_stage_suggests_interview_prep | planned |

## E1. Boards (LinkedIn, freehire, Jobbank) and add-portal

# E1 parity slice: job-board skills (linkedin, freehire, jobbank) and /add-portal

SUMMARY
1. Files read in full: for each of linkedin-search, freehire-search, jobbank-search the SKILL.md, url-reference.md (linkedin, freehire, jobbank all have one), cli/README.md, every cli/src/**/*.ts and every cli/tests/*.ts plus linkedin's fixtures; also .claude/commands/add-portal.md and tests/test_add_portal_command.py. Paths in the tables are relative to the original checkout root; a bare cli/... path is under the skill named in the same cell.
2. 189 rows (mostly planned; ~10 marked not ported because they are human-facing table/plain views, framework help, or bun packaging).
3. The matrix cannot express: per-board live behaviour (Cloudflare walls, challenge pages, rate-limit headers) and selector drift; those live in the knowledge notes boards/linkedin.md, boards/freehire.md, boards/jobbank.md, and adapter fixtures are described there.
4. Surprising: (a) add-portal's only test is a string-presence check on one prose line, so every other add-portal guarantee (robots, auth wall, mandatory live test, personal-use banner) is prose-only with no test. (b) LinkedIn's robots.txt (checked live 2026-10-19) disallows everything for generic crawlers and names /jobs-guest/ explicitly, so the new robots gate will block the LinkedIn adapter; the original never checked robots. (c) jobbank was unreachable (Cloudflare 403 even for robots.txt) and its README claims a browser UA while the code sends an honest one; its --company flag is a numeric id in the CLI but a name in url-reference; its validation errors use a different JSON shape from every other error.
5. Contradictions pinned by tests: linkedin SKILL lists age values 1/7/14/30 but tests and code accept any whole number >= 1; the tests treat "-5" as an unknown flag (UNKNOWN_FLAG), not BAD_ARG, as a deliberate consequence of the flag parser.

Planned module names used: boards/contract (shared result shape, validation, error format), boards/http (fetch helper: UA, timeout, backoff), boards/linkedin, boards/freehire, boards/jobbank, guards.robots, search.filters (client-side filtering), addboard.

| Unexpected exceptions that escape a command are caught at top level and reported as code INTERNAL_ERROR with exit 1, never a stack trace (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:206-215; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:274-284) | boards/contract.main | test_uncaught_exception_becomes_internal_error_json | planned |
| Search success output is a JSON object with a meta block and a results list (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:74-80; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:141-147; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:174) | boards/contract.SearchOutput | test_search_output_has_meta_and_results_for_each_adapter | planned (stricter: all three boards emit identical meta keys count, page, total, where the original jobbank omitted count and page and linkedin omitted total) |

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| HTTP 429 and any 5xx are retried with exponential backoff: first delay 500 ms, doubling each time, plus random jitter of 0-500 ms (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:19-40; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:40-65; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:12-26) | boards/http.get | test_backoff_delays_double_with_jitter_on_429_and_5xx | planned |
| Backoff delay is capped: 8 seconds for linkedin and freehire, 5 seconds for jobbank (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:38; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:63; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:25) | boards/http.get | test_backoff_cap_per_board | planned |
| A 429 followed by a 200 succeeds on the second attempt (two calls in total) (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:33-43; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:34-44; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:37-47) | boards/http.get | test_retry_once_on_429_then_success | planned |
| Persistent 5xx gives up after the first attempt plus six retries, seven calls in total, and raises an error naming the status (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:53-59; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:54-60; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:57-63) | boards/http.get | test_gives_up_after_seven_attempts_on_persistent_500 | planned |
| Every request carries a 15 second timeout so a stalled connection cannot hang the caller (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:30; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:49; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:17; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-22; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-25; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-22) | boards/http.get | test_every_request_has_a_timeout | planned |
| A 404 is not retried: linkedin returns an empty body, freehire returns a null envelope, jobbank hands the response back to its caller (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:45-51; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:46-52; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:41) | boards/http.get | test_404_is_single_call_and_not_an_exception | planned |
| Other 4xx statuses (such as 403) are not retried; linkedin and freehire raise, jobbank returns the response so the caller can recognise a Cloudflare page (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:49-55; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:42-44) | boards/http.get | test_plain_4xx_is_not_retried | planned |
| A connection failure (refused, DNS, timeout) on freehire fails immediately with a clear message naming the base URL and does not retry, so an outage degrades the board quickly (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:51-56; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:62-71) | boards/http.get | test_connection_error_fails_fast_without_retry | planned |
| A 2xx response with a body that is not valid JSON is an error, not an empty success (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:70-75) | boards/freehire.parse_envelope | test_unparseable_2xx_body_is_error | planned |
| Requests are redirect-following (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:29; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:48) | boards/http.get | test_redirects_are_followed | planned |
| The User-Agent names the tool honestly, never a browser impersonation; the original used a compatible-style string naming the CLI, and freehire's string names project, version and contact URL (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:15; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:19-23; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:5; .claude/commands/add-portal.md:82) | boards/http.user_agent | test_user_agent_names_the_tool_and_has_contact_url | planned (stricter: one shared honest UA with a contact URL for every board; the original linkedin and jobbank strings had none) |
| Jobbank's request header must equal its declared USER_AGENT constant, and its README must not claim a browser UA the code does not send (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:15-37; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:9, 361) | boards/http.user_agent | test_sent_user_agent_equals_declared_constant | planned |
| No board request is made if argument validation fails; invalid input fails before any network call (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:40-49; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/required-search-values.test.ts:4-30; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/missing-option-values.test.ts:24-42) | boards/contract.validate | test_invalid_args_make_no_request | planned |
| The research and runtime fetch must pass a robots.txt check before scraping HTML pages (not present in the original boards; the add-portal spec requires it at design time) (.claude/commands/add-portal.md:40, 82) | guards.robots.allowed | test_html_board_requires_robots_allow_before_fetch | planned (stricter: the original never checked robots in the CLIs; the loop enforces RFC 9309 in code, and a robots.txt that is itself a challenge page counts as blocked) |
| Minimum politeness: the original added a 300 ms pause before jobbank's secondary count request and documented 300-500 ms between sequential calls; others had none (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:142-143; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:398-400) | boards/http.RateGate | test_min_gap_between_requests_to_one_host | planned (stricter: every board gets a minimum gap and a per-run request cap) |

## LinkedIn: flag validation and request construction

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| search requires a location; missing gives code NO_LOCATION and exit 1 (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:105-115; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:128-134) | boards/linkedin.validate | test_linkedin_search_without_location_is_error | planned |
| Unknown flags are rejected with code UNKNOWN_FLAG naming the flag, exit 1, empty stdout, before any request; flags are never silently dropped because a dropped filter changes results (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:86-103; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:176-183) | boards/contract.validate | test_unknown_flag_is_rejected_loudly | planned |
| Age, minutes, page and limit must be whole numbers of at least 1; text, fractions (1.5, 0.5) and zero give BAD_ARG naming the flag; fractions are rejected not truncated (a truncated 0.5 became 0 and dropped the filter) (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:134-146; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:16-22, 45-69) | boards/contract.parse_positive_int | test_positive_int_flags_reject_text_fraction_and_zero | planned |
| A numeric flag given with no value is rejected with exit 1 and a stderr message (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:24-28) | boards/contract.parse_positive_int | test_numeric_flag_without_value_fails | planned |
| A value that starts with a dash (for example minutes of -5) is read as a stray flag and fails with UNKNOWN_FLAG, so a negative number never produces a silent unfiltered search (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:26-31; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:81-94) | boards/contract.validate | test_negative_number_fails_loudly | planned |
| Giving both the days window and the minutes window fails with CONFLICTING_AGE_FLAGS (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:124-132; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:96-105) | boards/linkedin.validate | test_days_and_minutes_conflict_is_error | planned (stricter: the rebuilt contract has a single recency value so the conflict cannot occur) |
| Format must be one of json, table, plain for search and json, plain for detail; otherwise BAD_ARG (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:116-122, 188-194; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:150-167) | boards/contract.validate | test_format_value_validated | not ported, because the loop does not expose table or plain formats |
| Posted-within is sent as f_TPR with the letter r and the number of seconds: days times 86400 (7 days gives r604800), minutes times 60 (30 gives r1800, 1440 equals one day); non-positive input or the 9999-day sentinel sends no parameter (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:267-276; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:214-225; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:30-40; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/search.test.ts:43-60) | boards/linkedin.build_params | test_recency_days_maps_to_f_tpr_seconds | planned |
| Workplace type maps remote to 2, hybrid to 3, onsite or on-site to 1 (case-insensitive); an unknown value sends nothing (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:279-291) | boards/linkedin.build_params | test_workplace_flag_maps_to_f_wt | planned (stricter: an unknown workplace value is an error, not a dropped filter) |
| Pagination offset is (page minus 1) times 10 and start is always sent, even for page 1 (start=0) (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:31; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:135-148) | boards/linkedin.build_params | test_page_offset_is_ten_per_page | planned |
| Keywords and location are sent as keywords and location query parameters; the location string passes through unchanged (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:25-26; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:30-40) | boards/linkedin.build_params | test_location_and_keywords_pass_through | planned |
| Limit is a client-side cap applied after parsing; limit 0 at the function level yields zero results (the CLI rejects 0) (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:60; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/search.test.ts:22-41) | boards/linkedin.search | test_limit_slices_results_client_side | planned |
| Search meta reports the count of results emitted and the page (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:76; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:135-148) | boards/linkedin.search | test_meta_count_and_page | planned |
| Search failure (network or HTTP) is reported as SEARCH_FAILED, exit 1 (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:83-86) | boards/linkedin.search | test_search_failure_code | planned |
| The skill is for personal use only: it must carry a visible warning that automated access is against LinkedIn's terms, keep volume low, no commercial or bulk use (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/SKILL.md:29-33; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/README.md:10-12; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/url-reference.md:6; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:6-8) | boards/linkedin.notice | test_linkedin_enable_requires_personal_use_acknowledgement | planned (stricter: the adapter refuses to run until the user has acknowledged the notice in config, and the robots disallow for LinkedIn is logged and overridden only by that explicit opt-in) |
| Skill is enabled by default in the original; a disabled flag keeps a portal installed but skipped by the scrape (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/SKILL.md:14) | search.sources.enabled | test_disabled_board_is_skipped | planned |

## LinkedIn: search card parsing

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| The search response is split on the job-posting URN marker and each card is parsed independently so one malformed card cannot break the rest (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:137-146) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_malformed_card_does_not_break_others | planned |
| id is the digits after urn:li:jobPosting: ; a chunk with no leading digits is skipped (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:147-149) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_card_without_digits_is_skipped | planned |
| Title comes from the card title heading, falling back to the screen-reader-only span; a card with neither is dropped (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:155-162) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_title_fallback_and_drop_when_missing | planned |
| Company comes from the subtitle heading text, company URL from its link with the query string removed; both null when absent (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:164-172) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_company_and_company_url_extracted | planned |
| Location comes from the card location span and is null when absent or empty (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:174-176; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:46-55) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_location_extracted_or_null | planned |
| Date comes from the datetime attribute of the list-date time element, including the --new class variant, and is null when absent (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:177-178; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:33-56) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_date_from_listdate_both_class_variants | planned |
| The result URL is the full-link href with entities decoded and the query string cut; if there is no link it is built from the id as https://www.linkedin.com/jobs/view/<id> (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:152-153, 187) | boards/linkedin.parse_cards | test_url_strips_tracking_and_has_fallback | planned |
| HTML entities in titles and company are decoded, including decimal and hex numeric references with upper or lower case x, supplementary-plane code points (emoji), dropping out-of-range values instead of raising (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:100-121; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:58-88) | boards/linkedin.clean_text | test_entity_decoding_numeric_hex_and_emoji | planned |
| Tags are stripped and whitespace collapsed in extracted text (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:123-129) | boards/linkedin.clean_text | test_strip_tags_and_collapse_whitespace | planned |
| Parsing a saved real response (two verbatim cards) yields the expected ids, limited by the limit flag, with the exact request parameters asserted (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:30-40, 135-148; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/fixtures/search-cards.provenance.md:1-19) | boards/linkedin.search | test_saved_real_search_response_parses_expected_ids | planned |
| Test fixtures are saved verbatim from real responses with capture date and hash, and tests never contact LinkedIn (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/fixtures/search-cards.provenance.md:16-19; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/fixtures/README.md:18-20) | tests/fixtures | test_fixture_provenance_recorded_and_no_network | planned |

## LinkedIn: detail parsing and active detection

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| detail requires an id or URL; none gives NO_ID, exit 1 (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/cli.ts:182-187) | boards/linkedin.validate | test_detail_without_id_is_error | planned |
| detail accepts a bare id of 6 or more digits, a urn:li:jobPosting URN, a LinkedIn job-view URL (any linkedin.com host, with or without scheme, with or without slug, trailing slash, tracking query) or a bare title slug ending in the id (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:22-44; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:227-273) | boards/linkedin.normalize_id | test_normalize_id_accepts_all_documented_forms | planned |
| detail rejects a job URL on any other host (Greenhouse, Lever, example.com), look-alike hosts (linkedin.com.evil.io, notlinkedin.com), userinfo tricks (linkedin.com@evil.io), linkedin.com URLs that are not job views, ids under 6 digits, and empty input (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:8-39; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:275-299) | boards/linkedin.normalize_id | test_normalize_id_rejects_foreign_and_lookalike_hosts | planned |
| A rejected id exits 1 with BAD_ID and makes no network request (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:47-51; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:39-49, 65-72) | boards/linkedin.detail | test_foreign_url_rejected_before_any_request | planned |
| A valid LinkedIn URL leads to a request for the detail endpoint with exactly the extracted id (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:51-62) | boards/linkedin.detail | test_detail_request_url_built_from_extracted_id | planned |
| Detail 404 is reported as NOT_FOUND, exit 1 (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:53-57) | boards/linkedin.detail | test_detail_404_is_not_found | planned |
| Other detail failures are DETAIL_FAILED, exit 1 (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:80-83) | boards/linkedin.detail | test_detail_failure_code | planned |
| The description is taken from the show-more-less markup block (or description text block) using a depth-aware extractor that handles nested divs, returning empty string for an empty block and nothing when absent (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:69-98, 210-223; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:156-212) | boards/linkedin.extract_div | test_nested_div_extraction | planned |
| Description keeps paragraph and list boundaries as newlines (br and closing p, li, ul, ol, div, headings), cleans each line, collapses 3+ newlines to 2, contains no raw tags (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:215-222; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-description-cli.test.ts:52-63) | boards/linkedin.description_text | test_description_keeps_paragraph_and_list_boundaries | planned |
| Job criteria (seniority level, employment type, job function, industries) are read from subheader and value pairs keyed by lower-cased label; missing is null (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:225-232, 258-261; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-description-cli.test.ts:65-74) | boards/linkedin.parse_detail | test_criteria_fields_extracted | planned |
| Detail title falls back to "(untitled)", company and URL from the org link, location from the bullet flavor span, the title decodes entities (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:195-208, 251; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:148-154) | boards/linkedin.parse_detail | test_detail_title_company_location | planned |
| Closed detection looks only at the part of the page before the description starts, for the closed-job flavor class or the phrase "no longer accepting applications"; recruiter boilerplate or CSS class names inside the description must not mark a live job closed (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:234-247; .agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:90-133) | boards/linkedin.is_closed | test_closed_banner_only_in_top_card | planned |
| isActive true means only that no closed banner was found, not proof the job is open; a consent wall or markup drift also shows no banner (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:241-244) | boards/linkedin.is_closed | test_active_is_tristate_with_unknown_for_empty_page | planned (stricter: closed, no-banner, and unknown are distinct states; an empty or challenge body is unknown) |
| A saved real closed-posting response yields isActive false, the exact title, seniority Mid-Senior level, employment Contract, and plain output shows "Status: CLOSED / EXPIRED" (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/detail-description-cli.test.ts:52-74) | boards/linkedin.parse_detail | test_saved_closed_posting_fixture | planned |
| The detail output has no applyUrl field; the removed field must not return (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:135-146) | boards/linkedin.parse_detail | test_detail_has_no_apply_url_field | planned |
| Detail date is null on this endpoint and the URL is built as https://www.linkedin.com/jobs/view/<id> (.agents/skills/linkedin-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:255-256) | boards/linkedin.parse_detail | test_detail_date_null_and_canonical_url | planned |

## freehire: flag validation and request construction

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unknown flags are rejected with UNKNOWN_FLAG, empty stdout, before any request (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:153-170; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:201-216) | boards/contract.validate | test_freehire_unknown_flag_rejected | planned |
| Age, page and limit must be whole numbers of at least 1; text, fractions and zero give BAD_ARG naming the flag (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:116-130, 205-211; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:16-51) | boards/contract.parse_positive_int | test_freehire_positive_int_flags | planned |
| Value-taking flags (query, company, country, region, city, seniority, category, skill, description-format) given absent, empty or whitespace-only values fail with BAD_ARG naming the flag and make no request, because treating them as unset silently broadens the query (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:173-183; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/required-search-values.test.ts:4-30) | boards/contract.validate | test_empty_filter_values_rejected_before_request | planned |
| Format must be json, table or plain for search and json or plain for detail, else BAD_ARG (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:185-191, 259-265; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:153-169) | boards/contract.validate | test_freehire_format_validated | not ported, because the loop does not expose table or plain formats |
| Description format must be markdown, text or html, validated client-side because the server silently falls back to html on an unknown value (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:193-203; .agents/skills/freehire-search/url-reference.md:47; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:80-88) | boards/freehire.validate | test_description_format_validated_client_side | planned |
| The --facet flag takes key=value, is repeatable, comma-separated values split and trimmed; no equals sign, empty key, or no non-empty values gives BAD_ARG naming --facet and makes no request, including a bare --facet after a valid one (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:39-49, 213-229; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:90-151) | boards/freehire.parse_facets | test_facet_flag_validation_and_repeat | planned |
| Repeated facet values reach the API as repeated query parameters in order (countries US, CA, GB), plus other facets like salary_min and work_mode (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:54-67; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:121-145) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_facets_become_repeated_query_params | planned |
| Comma-separated named facets (country, skill) are split and trimmed (for example "DE, GB" gives DE and GB) and sent as repeated parameters (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:65-72; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/required-search-values.test.ts:32-62) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_comma_lists_split_into_repeated_params | planned |
| A bare --remote means work mode remote; --remote with a value uses that value (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:59-63, 246-247; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:134-141) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_bare_remote_means_remote | planned (stricter: only remote, hybrid, onsite accepted) |
| Search is offset based: limit defaults to 25, offset is (page minus 1) times limit, both always sent (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:47-48; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:234-235; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:53-78) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_limit_and_offset_computation | planned |
| posted_within_days is sent only for an age above 0 and below the 9999 sentinel (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:50; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:53-78) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_recency_maps_to_posted_within_days | planned |
| Named filters map to API parameters: region to regions, country to countries, city to cities, seniority, category, skill to skills, company to company_slug, remote to work_mode, query to q (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:46-67; .agents/skills/freehire-search/url-reference.md:63-79; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/required-search-values.test.ts:32-62) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_named_filters_map_to_api_params | planned |
| Repeated values of one facet are ORed and different facets ANDed; geography ORs into one location group; deep paging is bounded server side at offset plus limit of 10000 (.agents/skills/freehire-search/url-reference.md:81-83) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_pagination_guard_offset_plus_limit_10000 | planned (stricter: the adapter refuses a page beyond that bound instead of letting the server answer) |
| The region value none selects jobs whose region could not be resolved and ORs with real regions; a missing region means unresolved, not "not applicable" (.agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:104-105, 172-186) | boards/freehire.build_params | test_region_none_bucket_supported | planned |
| Facet values are never invented; they come from the facets endpoint (.agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:114-119, 195-197) | boards/freehire.facets | test_facet_values_validated_against_facet_endpoint | planned (stricter: the adapter checks values against a cached facets response and rejects unknown ones; the original only advised this) |
| The base URL defaults to https://freehire.me and can be overridden by FREEHIRE_API_URL, blank means default, trailing slashes stripped (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:7-13; .agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:51-66) | boards/freehire.base_url | test_base_url_env_override_and_trailing_slash | planned |
| Freehire is a best-effort third-party service with no SLA; unreachable means a clear non-zero failure, not a hang (.agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:41-50; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/README.md:10-15) | boards/freehire.search | test_outage_is_reported_as_unavailable_board | planned |
| Reads are public and the adapter never uses the per-user tracking endpoints (apply, save, me) (.agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:190-192; .agents/skills/freehire-search/url-reference.md:7-11) | boards/freehire | test_adapter_only_calls_read_endpoints | planned (stricter: an allow-list of paths enforced in the http layer) |

|---|---|---|---|

| A 404 from the search endpoint is an error naming the endpoint path and suggesting an upgrade or unsetting the base URL, never an empty result set (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:117-126; .agents/skills/freehire-search/url-reference.md:53-55; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/commands.test.ts:164-178) | boards/freehire.search | test_search_404_is_error_not_empty | planned |
| Search meta carries count, page and total, where total falls back to the row count when the API gives none (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:134, 143; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/commands.test.ts:90-102) | boards/freehire.search | test_meta_count_page_total | planned |

| detail requires a slug or URL (NO_ID), parses a bare slug or /jobs/<slug> URL, rejects anything else with BAD_ID before any request (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/cli.ts:253-258; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:249-258; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:27-31; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:171-184; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:139-150) | boards/freehire.normalize_slug | test_normalize_slug_forms_and_rejections | planned (stricter: a URL input must be on the freehire host) |

| Detail description is HTML turned into readable text: breaks at block closes and br, tags removed, entities decoded (including hex), spaces collapsed, 3+ newlines to 2, null when empty (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:236-247; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:74-77, 109-120; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/commands.test.ts:209-220) | boards/freehire.clean_html | test_detail_html_to_text | planned |

| Salary is a single string: currency, min, en dash, max, plus a slash and the period when known; a single bound prints one number; null when both bounds are missing; a yearly figure must never be readable as monthly (.agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:203-213; .agents/skills/freehire-search/SKILL.md:128-130; .agents/skills/freehire-search/cli/tests/parsing.test.ts:79-100) | boards/freehire.format_salary | test_salary_keeps_period | planned |

## jobbank: flags and request construction

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| search needs a keyword or at least one filter; otherwise MISSING_REQUIRED with the exact message "--key or at least one filter is required", exit 1, empty stdout (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:82-100; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-contract.test.ts:9-18) | boards/jobbank.validate | test_search_without_any_filter_is_missing_required | planned |

| Limit must be a whole number of at least 1; -1, 0 and 1.5 are rejected so a negative limit cannot silently drop the last result (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:72-74; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:21-36) | boards/contract.parse_positive_int | test_jobbank_limit_validation | planned |
| The company filter must be a whole number of at least 1; negative and fractional values are rejected rather than sent raw (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:63-65; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:38-48) | boards/jobbank.validate | test_company_id_must_be_positive_int | planned |
| A valid limit passes validation (proven by reaching the next guard) (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:50-58) | boards/jobbank.validate | test_valid_limit_accepted | planned |
| Unknown long flags and unknown short flags (including another board's --query or -q) are rejected with UNKNOWN_FLAG naming the flag, empty stdout, because the framework would otherwise drop them and return the whole database as a successful search (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/cli.ts:17-61; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:62-95) | boards/contract.validate | test_jobbank_unknown_long_and_short_flags_rejected | planned |
| A dash-prefixed value (negative number) after a flag is rejected as UNKNOWN_FLAG instead of falling back to a default (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/cli.ts:23-32; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:97-104) | boards/contract.validate | test_negative_value_not_swallowed_by_default | planned |
| -h still prints help with exit 0 and nothing on stderr; the --flag=value form is the way to pass a value starting with a dash (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/cli.ts:39-44; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:106-110; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/missing-option-values.test.ts:58-62) | boards/contract.main | test_help_flag_exits_zero | not ported, because the loop has no CLI help for adapters |
| Every value-taking option (remote, type, company, since) given with no value fails with BAD_ARG "option ... requires a value" before any fetch, so the filter is not silently dropped (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/cli.ts:62-80; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/missing-option-values.test.ts:27-42) | boards/contract.validate | test_option_without_value_is_bad_arg_before_fetch | planned |
| Valued options still reach the request: remote helt becomes fjernarbejde=helt, and --type=3,6 becomes cvtype=3&cvtype=6 (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/missing-option-values.test.ts:44-56) | boards/jobbank.build_params | test_valued_options_reach_the_request | planned |
| Repeatable filters (type, education, location, work-area, industry, suitable-for) are sent as repeated parameters cvtype, udd, amt, erf, branche, andet in the same order, in both the RSS and the count requests (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:105-128; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/repeated-filter-cli.test.ts:56-71) | boards/jobbank.build_params | test_repeatable_filters_preserved_in_both_requests | planned |
| Comma lists and repeats merge in order (--type=3,6 with --type=13 gives 3, 6, 13); a single value and a comma list keep their request shape (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/repeated-filter-cli.test.ts:73-84) | boards/jobbank.build_params | test_comma_and_repeat_merge | planned |
| Single-value filters map: key to key, exclude to antikey, company to virk, remote to fjernarbejde, since to oprettet (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:103-131; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/url-reference.md:24-37) | boards/jobbank.build_params | test_single_value_filters_map | planned |
| remote must be helt or delvist, since must be YYYY-MM-DD (documented values; the original did not validate either) (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/url-reference.md:35-36; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:66-71) | boards/jobbank.validate | test_remote_and_since_values_validated | planned (stricter: invalid values rejected in code) |

## jobbank: RSS fetch and parsing

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Search uses the RSS feed endpoint, at most 100 items, with no pagination (the page parameter has no effect on RSS) (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/SKILL.md:65; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:209-210) | boards/jobbank.search | test_rss_cap_100_and_no_paging | planned |
| Parameters are serialised with repeated keys, path /job/rss, and the request carries the declared User-Agent (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:79-91; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:15-46) | boards/jobbank.build_params | test_rss_request_shape | planned |
| A 403 whose body mentions just a moment, cloudflare or cf-chl is reported as Cloudflare bot protection with advice to skip the portal and fall back to web search, and is never retried (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:92-100; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-fetch.test.ts:10-20; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/SKILL.md:28, 176) | boards/jobbank.search | test_cloudflare_403_means_board_unavailable | planned (stricter: the board is marked unavailable for the whole run and the loop uses another route; no retry or evasion) |
| Any other non-OK RSS response is "Failed to fetch RSS feed: status text" (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:99) | boards/jobbank.search | test_other_rss_failure_message | planned |
| RSS items are split per item and parsed independently; values are read from CDATA first and plain text second (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:40-77; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:15-46) | boards/jobbank.parse_rss | test_rss_cdata_and_plain_fields | planned |
| The link is read as a text node of the link element, not confused with an Atom link (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:51-62) | boards/jobbank.parse_rss | test_link_text_node_extracted | planned |
| The job id is the first numeric segment after /job/ in the item link; a non-numeric segment gives an empty id (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:162-172; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:91-96) | boards/jobbank.job_id_from_url | test_id_from_link | planned (stricter: an item with an empty id is dropped or flagged, not emitted as empty) |
| The RSS description "types hos Company, Location (Ansøgningsfrist: ...)" is parsed into job types (possibly several), company, location and deadline, splitting at the first " hos " and the first ", " (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:112-160; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:49-61) | boards/jobbank.parse_description | test_description_split_into_parts | planned |
| A deadline of DD.MM.YYYY is converted to YYYY-MM-DD (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:138-143; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:49-61) | boards/jobbank.parse_description | test_deadline_dmy_to_iso | planned |
| A rolling deadline (løbende) becomes null (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:135-136; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:70-79) | boards/jobbank.parse_description | test_rolling_deadline_null | planned |
| An unrecognised deadline text passes through unchanged so downstream code can see it (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:141-142; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:63-68) | boards/jobbank.parse_description | test_unknown_deadline_passthrough | planned (stricter: the rebuilt contract returns a separate deadline_raw and a null deadline for non-dates) |
| An unstructured description with no " hos " is kept as the company fallback with other fields empty/null and never raises (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/helpers.ts:122-126; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/rss-parsing.test.ts:81-88) | boards/jobbank.parse_description | test_unstructured_description_fallback | planned |
| The posted timestamp is the pubDate converted to ISO UTC, and date is its first 10 characters (for example Fri 14 Aug 2026 09:30 +0200 gives 2026-08-14T07:30:00.000Z and 2026-08-14) (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:13-25; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/search-normalization.test.ts:15-21) | boards/jobbank.normalize_item | test_pubdate_to_iso_and_date | planned |
| A missing or unparseable pubDate yields posted empty and date null and must not fail the whole search; one bad item cannot kill the run (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:8-15; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/search-normalization.test.ts:23-43) | boards/jobbank.normalize_item | test_bad_pubdate_degrades_to_null | planned |
| Normalised items keep the native fields company, location, url and deadline next to the contract date (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:15-26; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/search-normalization.test.ts:45-52) | boards/jobbank.normalize_item | test_native_fields_kept | planned |
| meta.total comes from a secondary HTML request whose title matches "<N> relevante job"; thousands separators removed; any failure there leaves total null and does not fail the search (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:139-164; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:272) | boards/jobbank.count | test_total_from_title_and_null_on_failure | planned |
| A search makes at most two requests (RSS plus count) with a short pause between them (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:398-400; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/src/commands/search.ts:142-143) | boards/jobbank.search | test_at_most_two_requests_per_search | planned |
| Total reflects the true number of matches while results are capped at 100 (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/SKILL.md:65, 177; .agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/README.md:210) | boards/jobbank.search | test_total_independent_of_cap | planned |
| Validation failures from the framework are emitted in a different JSON shape (ok false, error with kind, option, message) than the board's own errors (.agents/skills/jobbank-search/cli/tests/cli-flag-validation.test.ts:11-18) | boards/contract.write_error | test_all_errors_share_one_shape | planned (stricter: a single error shape error and code for every failure) |

## /add-portal command (addboard)

## E2. Danish boards (Jobindex, Jobnet, Jobdanmark)

# Parity slice E2: Danish boards (jobindex, jobnet, jobdanmark)

1. Files read completely: for each of `.agents/skills/jobindex-search`, `jobnet-search`, `jobdanmark-search` (below `JI`, `JN`, `JD`): SKILL.md, README, every `cli/src/**/*.ts`, every `cli/tests/*.ts`; plus JI/url-reference.md. Live probes (2026-10-19) are in the board notes, not cited as rows.
2. Row count: 139 (table rows, excluding headers).
3. Not expressible in the matrix: the per-board endpoint/selector knowledge (see `boards/jobindex.md`, `boards/jobnet.md`, `boards/jobdanmark.md`); the original's `--format table|plain` renderers and bunli CLI plumbing are deliberately not ported (the loop calls adapters as functions).
4. Surprises: (a) the originals have no robots.txt check and jobindex's live robots.txt disallows the `page=`, `sort=`, `jobage=` params the CLI always sends; jobnet's robots.txt redirects to a login (307); (b) jobdanmark's "not found" detection is dead against the live site (missing slug 301-redirects to a 200 search page) and live pages have no JSON-LD; (c) a fresh jobindex detail page matches neither parsed shape, so company/location/deadline would come back null; (d) several guarantees are prose-only: jobnet "do not combine --region and --postal-code", "BestMatch requires a search string", jobindex "jobage is 1/7/14/30/9999" (schema accepts any integer >= 1), and sort/order values are unvalidated strings; jobindex's `apiFetch` and its two tests (timeout, retry) cover code the CLI never calls (search uses `htmlFetch`).
5. Contradictions: jobindex url-reference.md says path-based area slugs work, README/SKILL say area filtering does not work and the CLI never builds those paths; jobindex README says detail "follows redirects to the canonical URL" but the code always fetches the rebuilt short URL; jobnet README says `--query` is required in an error example but the flag is `--search-string`.

Legend: `JI` = `.agents/skills/jobindex-search`, `JN` = `.agents/skills/jobnet-search`, `JD` = `.agents/skills/jobdanmark-search`. Cited line numbers are those of the original files. Planned module names: `boards/http` (shared httpx helper), `boards/contract` (shared normalisation and JSON contract), `boards/jobindex`, `boards/jobnet`, `boards/jobdanmark`, `guards` (robots), `search` (client-side filters).

### Shared HTTP behaviour (identical in all three CLIs)

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| Every request carries a hard 15 s timeout so a stalled upstream cannot hang the run (JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:17,46; JN/cli/src/helpers.ts:19; JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:16,48,84; JI/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-33; JN/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-22; JD/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:12-44) | boards/http.fetch | test_every_board_request_has_a_timeout_for_get_post_and_html | planned |
| A stalled connection must end in a clean adapter error (exit 1 / error result), never an infinite wait (JI/cli/tests/request-timeout.test.ts:4-6) | boards/http.fetch | test_timeout_raises_board_error_not_hang | planned |
| 429 and any 5xx are retried with exponential backoff: first delay 500 ms, doubling, capped at 5 s, plus 0-500 ms random jitter (JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:14-26; JN/cli/src/helpers.ts:11-30; JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:11-25) | boards/http.fetch | test_backoff_schedule_500ms_doubling_cap_5s_with_jitter | planned (stricter: also honours Retry-After) |
| Retries stop after the initial attempt plus six retries (seven calls total) and the last status is reported in the error text (JI/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:53-58,82-87; JN/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:52-57; JD/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:62-68; JD/cli/tests/detail-backoff.test.ts:105-118) | boards/http.fetch | test_persistent_5xx_gives_up_after_seven_calls_with_status_in_error | planned |
| A 429 followed by a 200 succeeds on the second call (JI/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:34-44; JN/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:33-43; JD/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:43-53) | boards/http.fetch | test_429_then_200_returns_second_response | planned |
| Plain 4xx (400, others except 404 handling) is never retried: exactly one call, error contains the status (JI/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:46-51; JN/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:45-50; JD/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:55-60) | boards/http.fetch | test_plain_4xx_is_not_retried | planned |
| The retry policy applies identically to GET-JSON, POST-JSON and GET-HTML paths; separate copies must not drift (JI/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:4-8; JD/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:4-10,35-39) | boards/http.fetch | test_one_retry_policy_shared_by_json_post_and_html_calls | planned (stricter: a single function so drift is impossible) |
| The detail path must retry on 429 through the same wrapper, not a bare fetch (the earlier bug failed on the first 429 at detail time) (JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:67-74; JD/cli/tests/detail-backoff.test.ts:86-103) | boards/jobdanmark.detail | test_detail_retries_429_and_returns_posting | planned |
| Every request sends an identifying User-Agent rather than the HTTP library default (JN/cli/src/helpers.ts:2,16; JN/cli/tests/user-agent.test.ts:13-26; JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:2,15,45,82; JD/cli/tests/user-agent.test.ts:23-61) | boards/http.fetch | test_user_agent_header_present_on_get_post_and_html | planned (stricter: honest UA naming the loop and contact, one constant for all boards) |
| JI's HTML fetch sent a browser-like UA with Accept-Language `da,en;q=0.9`; its JSON helper sent no UA at all (JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:7-17,39-46) | boards/http.fetch | test_no_board_request_goes_out_without_user_agent | planned (stricter: no UA-less path exists) |
| HTML fetches ask for HTML via an Accept header and follow redirects (JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:41-45; JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:81) | boards/http.fetch | test_html_fetch_sends_accept_html | planned |
| JSON POST requests send `Content-Type: application/json` (JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:44; JD/cli/tests/user-agent.test.ts:36-48) | boards/jobdanmark.search | test_post_sends_json_content_type | planned |
| JN requests always carry header `x-csrf: 1`; without it the API refuses (JN/cli/src/helpers.ts:17; JN/SKILL.md:201; JN/cli/README.md:383) | boards/jobnet | test_every_jobnet_request_has_x_csrf_header | planned |
| A 404 on an HTML posting is reported as not-found immediately, never retried (JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:57-59; JD/cli/src/helpers.ts:95-97; JD/cli/tests/retry-backoff.test.ts:72-78; JD/cli/tests/detail-backoff.test.ts:120-133) | boards/http.fetch | test_html_404_is_not_found_without_retry | planned |
| The original never consults robots.txt; the rebuild must check it (RFC 9309) before any HTML or API fetch and report "blocked by robots.txt" distinctly from "no results" (no original source; added requirement; JI/cli/src/helpers.ts:35-66) | guards.robots + boards/http | test_disallowed_url_is_reported_blocked_not_empty | planned (stricter: not enforced by original) |
| JI's live robots.txt disallows `page=`, `sort=`, `jobage=` on `/jobsoegning`, so those params must be dropped or the fetch skipped when the live file forbids them (JI/cli/src/commands/search.ts:37-45) | boards/jobindex.search + guards.robots | test_jobindex_params_blocked_by_robots_are_applied_client_side | planned (stricter: not enforced by original) |
| An unreadable robots.txt that redirects to a login (JN) is surfaced as a decision, never treated silently as permission (no original source; JN/cli/src/helpers.ts:4-40) | guards.robots | test_robots_auth_redirect_is_reported_not_assumed_allowed | planned (stricter: not enforced by original) |

|---|---|---|---|

| Original guarantee (file:line) | Where it lives here | How it's checked (test name) | Status |

| The fetch URL is rebuilt from the extracted id (`https://www.jobindex.dk/jobannonce/{id}`), never the raw input; slug, trailing slash and query string are ignored; subdomain and bare apex hosts accepted (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:87-89; JI/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:11-26; JI/cli/README.md:218) | boards/jobindex.build_url | test_url_rebuilt_from_id_for_slug_query_subdomain_variants | planned |
| An own-host URL without a jobannonce id (the homepage) is rejected, so a non-posting page can never come back as a fake posting with exit 0 (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:62-75; JI/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:44-46) | boards/jobindex.build_url | test_homepage_url_is_bad_id_not_fake_posting | planned |
| A bare id is a scheme-free, slash-free token of letters, digits, `_`, `-`; garbage and non-http schemes are rejected (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:91-94; JI/cli/tests/detail-input.test.ts:28-33,48-51) | boards/jobindex.build_url | test_bare_id_accepted_garbage_and_ftp_rejected | planned |
| Detail output `id` and `url` are always the jobindex id and its jobannonce URL, never the page's og:url or canonical (which point at an external ATS on passthrough pages) (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:168-171,247-258; JI/cli/README.md:183-185; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:120-124) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_detail_url_never_replaced_by_og_url | planned |
| A page is classified native when it contains `jd-` facts blocks; native pages give company from the title prefix, location, deadline, job type, hours and description (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:173,187-210; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:92-114) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_native_page_extracts_contract_fields | planned |
| Company is taken from the `<title>` prefix only when the suffix equals the job title, so an unrelated title never becomes a company (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:184-194) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_company_requires_title_suffix_match | planned |
| On external-ATS passthrough pages company is null, never the ATS brand (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:160-166; JI/cli/README.md:176-181; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:126-129) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_passthrough_company_is_null | planned |

| Passthrough deadline is found by label (Ansøgningsfrist, Application due, Frist) plus a real date within 300 characters, scanned only over visible markup; a CSS comment containing the label must not be captured, and no widget means null (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:220-226; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:135-137,144-151) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_deadline_ignores_css_comment_and_is_null_without_widget | planned |
| Deadlines are normalised from ISO, `DD-MM-YYYY` and Danish long form ("13. september 2026", optional dot) to `YYYY-MM-DD`, else null (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:97-120; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:106-108) | boards/jobindex.to_iso_date | test_to_iso_date_three_formats_and_garbage | planned |
| Description is readable body text (head, script, style, comments dropped), never stylesheet text; when it is under 100 characters on passthrough pages the meta description replaces it; falls back to og:description when empty (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:138-154,228-236; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:139-142) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_description_is_visible_text_not_css | planned |
| Danish named entities (`&oslash; &aelig; &aring;` and capitals) decode in titles and bodies (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:42-48; JI/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:127) | boards/jobindex (html.unescape) | test_danish_named_entities_decoded | planned |
| Title is og:title, else itemprop name, else the first h1; none at all is PARSE_ERROR (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:175-182,300-308) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_title_fallback_chain_and_parse_error | planned |
| Meta tags are matched with `property`/`name`/`itemprop` before or after `content` (JI/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:122-130) | boards/jobindex.detail | test_meta_attribute_order_does_not_matter | planned |

| JSON-LD HTML descriptions are rendered to plain text, one line per block element, entities decoded, whitespace collapsed; plain text is unchanged; absent is the empty string, so both branches give one description shape (JD/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:69-82; JD/cli/tests/detail-jsonld.test.ts:58-88) | boards/jobdanmark.parse_detail | test_jsonld_description_rendered_to_text_lines | planned |
| When no JobPosting JSON-LD exists, the rendered page is parsed: title (.title, h1, h3 minus " \| jobdanmark"), company link under /virksomheder/, logo, Udgivet, Jobtype, Arbejdssted, Ansøgningsfrist, description from p and li, apply link `a.action.primary` (JD/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:176-235; JD/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:35-54) | boards/jobdanmark.parse_detail | test_rendered_fallback_extracts_all_fields | planned |
| The fallback emits contract-shaped values: `DD-MM-YYYY` (even with a trailing time like "23.59") becomes ISO; the free text "Løbende" (rolling) becomes null (JD/cli/src/commands/detail.ts:148-161; JD/cli/tests/detail-parsing.test.ts:43-66) | boards/jobdanmark.normalise_overview_date | test_overview_dates_iso_and_rolling_is_null | planned |
